{"amberApp":1,"files":{"/README.md":"# Lift\n\nA strength-training logger: routines, a live workout with rest timer, an exercise library, history, charts, records and body measurements.\n\n## Screens\n- Workout (src/screens/home.tsx): start empty or from a routine; routines in folders, reorder via menu\n- Live workout (src/components/live-workout.tsx): sets with previous values, rest timer, supersets, finish summary\n- History (src/screens/history.tsx): month calendar, workout list, workout detail\n- Exercises (src/screens/exercises.tsx): ~100 exercises, search/filter, custom, per-exercise charts and records\n- Progress (src/screens/progress.tsx): weekly volume, muscle distribution, records, body measurements\n- Settings (src/screens/settings.tsx): units, rest defaults, RPE, plate calculator, data reset\n\n## Data\nStored as values in data.json. All weights are in kg internally; lengths in cm. The unit setting only changes display.\n- workouts: [{ id, name, start, end (ms), note?, routine?, exercises: [{ key, id (exercise id), ss?, rest?, note?, sets: [{ t: \"N\"|\"W\"|\"D\"|\"F\", w (kg), r (reps), rpe?, done }] }] }]\n- routines: [{ id, name, folder (folder id|null), note?, exercises: same shape as above }] (array order = display order)\n- folders: [{ id, name }] (array order = display order)\n- customExercises: [{ id, name, muscle, equip, custom: true }] (the built-in library lives in src/lib/data.ts)\n- measures: [{ id, date \"yyyy-MM-dd\", type: weight|fat|waist|chest|hips|arm|thigh, v }]\n- active: the workout in progress (or null), with rest: { end, total } while a rest timer runs\n- settings: { unit, rest, autoRest, rpe, bar: {kg, lb}, plates: {kg: [], lb: []} }\n- seeded: true once demo data has been created\n\n## How this app runs in Amber Notes\n\n- It is the note: opening the note opens the app, on iPhone (320-440 pt wide, safe areas, the keyboard shrinks the view) and on the Mac (a window from about 500 to 1,800 px, resized live). Light and dark follow the device.\n- A normal Vite + React 19 + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui project, compiled by Amber Notes on save.\n- Data is JSON that Amber Notes keeps (useStore from \"@/lib/amber\").\n","/index.html":"\n\n \n \n Lift\n \n \n

String(r2(n));\nconst fmtVol = (kg, unit) => {\n const v = dispW(kg, unit);\n return v >= 1e4 ? `${(v / 1e3).toFixed(1)}k` : Math.round(v).toLocaleString();\n};\nconst fmtDur = (sec) => {\n const m = w.exercises) {\n const d = e.sets.filter((s) => s.done);\n if (d.length) out[e.id] = d;\n }\n return out;\n}\nfunction prevForSet(prev, sets, idx) {\n if (!prev) return null;\n const isW = sets[idx].t {};\n const out = {};\n for (const wk of [...ws].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)) {\n const list = [];\n const local = {};\n for (const ex of wk.exercises)\n for (const s of ex.sets) {\n if (!s.done || !isWork(s) || s.r <= 0) continue;\n const m = local[_a = ex.id] ?? (local[_a] = { w: 0, e: 0, v: 0, reps: 0, sw: s, se: s, sv: s, sr: s });\n if (s.w > m.w) {\n m.w = s.w;\n m.sw = s;\n }\n if (est1rm(s.w, s.r) > m.e) {\n m.e = est1rm(s.w, s.r);\n m.se = s;\n }\n if (s.w * s.r > m.v) {\n m.v = s.w * s.r;\n m.sv = s;\n }\n if (s.w === 0 && s.r > m.reps) {\n m.reps = s.r;\n m.sr = s;\n }\n }\n for (const id in local) {\n const m = local[id], b = best[id];\n if (b) {\n if (m.w > b.w + 1e-6) list.push({ ex: id, kind: \"weight\", value: m.w, w: m.sw.w, r: m.sw.r });\n if (m.e > b.e + 1e-6 && !(m.w > b.w + 1e-6)) list.push({ ex: id, kind: \"1rm\", value: m.e, w: m.se.w, r: m.se.r });\n if (m.v > b.v + 1e-6 && m.w > 0 && !(m.w > b.w + 1e-6) && !(m.e > b.e + 1e-6)) list.push({ ex: id, kind: \"volume\", value: m.v, w: m.sv.w, r: m.sv.r });\n if (m.reps > b.reps && m.w === 0 && m.reps > 0 && b.w === 0) list.push({ ex: id, kind: \"reps\", value: m.reps, w: 0, r: m.reps });\n }\n const nb = best[id] ?? }\n return out;\n}\nfunction exSeries(ws, id) {\n const pts = [];\n for (const wk of [...ws].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)) {\n let best = 0, vol = 0, e = 0, reps = 0, any = false;\n for (const ex w = null, e = null, v = null, reps = 0, sessionVol = 0, count = 0, last = 0;\n for (const wk of ws) {\n let sv = 0, any = false;\n for (const ex of wk.exercises)\n if (ex.id === id)\n for (const s of > v.w * v.r) v = s;\n reps = Math.max(reps, s.r);\n sv += s.w * s.r;\n }\n if (any) {\n count++;\n sessionVol = Math.max(sessionVol, sv);\n last = Math.max(last, wk.start);\n }\n }\n return { w, e, v, null && cnt[e.ss] < 2 ? { ...e, ss: null } : e);\n}\nfunction newEx(id, rest) {\n return { key: uid(), id, sets: [{ t: \"N\", w: 0, r: 0 }], rest };\n}\nfunction rng(seed) {\n return () => {\n seed |=
